**R1.** Orthemology. [Recognition, Warrant and Restoration](../epistemology/recognition-warrant-and-restoration.md), especially §3. The current exposition remains a separate work; this chapter develops the normative and explanatory questions beyond its treatment of actual epistemic exercise.

**S23.** Orthemology public research. Source-relative premises and matched architecture comparison, revised edition. Exact Git object: `ee6e0bbf1db1cab0b25e75057e05060f5105324a`; source-authority dossier: `aec80bdcca9c20441d1471a226957505e37c43d7`; exact architecture and authority assignments: `2b5cafefc2e514c9e55bc81ef171287212f4b848`. The premise-relative argument, source distinctions, stronger alternatives and bounded comparison are used. The original translation and scholarly source snapshots, locator checks, historical comparison receipts and earlier dispositions retain their separate evidence identities; no fresh primary-source verification is claimed.

**S24.** Orthemology public research. Source-conditioned rival filtering and preservation of premise scope, revised edition. Exact Git object: `9586d802af9e9e7b53095cffb6cf73c2c31357be`; finite predicate assignments: `5d8672c214186160f1eb69b24cd5eb43f545eb17`; exact source-attribution note: `778f9b940e45fa714be9429ba26cf1b190a17d8f`. The finite separator, source-local co-predication and language restriction are treated here and in the existing incidence work. Source authentication, referent identification, translated-primary limits, the full-portfolio versus fixed-background counts, and original execution evidence remain distinct.

**S25.** Orthemology public research. Authority reconciliation and nonselection, fourth edition. Exact Git object: `c8b6aa2f1f51f5495ec84ece343afa3b17875f6b`. Exact finite comparison assignments: `6f3abf83b965468fe2b983d6bf5cf0d83e263159`. This is a corrected application and authority reconciliation of the earlier family, not an independent theorem origin.

**S26.** Orthemology public research. Authority-indexed anchor covers and guarded context migration, revised edition. Exact Git object: `7ef763b6658e5675679bdcd81742a0c49aec1edc`. The exact model assignments, initial failed identification criterion, and historical repair retain separate evidence identities.

**S27.** Orthemology public research. Registry-relative architecture separation and the personal–impersonal residual, revised edition. Exact Git object: `8fef2f536a4c571f86fd231e19a9923c9c6d287d`; typed anchor assignments: `9d4365ce30e5d4ba08f622ba925429ef58d83431`. The finite factorization, conditional residual, and type ceilings are used; their general proof is supplied by Proposition 5A. No independent operationalization, evidence-cost theorem, or exhaustive ontology is claimed. Historical checks retain their separate evidence identities.

**S28.** Orthemology public research. Source, formal model, and world: a guarded account of wise agency, revised edition. Exact Git object: `2953270dc2a123fe5776c92666a7037fcfdf3be3`; concrete neutral kernel: `ffc8b62232dfc42ce07ea1aa43d8d458f5a4e8d3`; source/world assignments and controls: `1f81a89d311d51b6aa77c24c7c0b2f371e80af43`; source-authority note: `45ba6a9c1673df83c13e647152d04b488d43c2e1`. The concrete experiment, finite-policy specialization, guarded positive argument, and scoped controls are used. The general controller-transfer credit belongs to the existing collective-inquiry treatment. Historical executions remain distinct from the present semantic arguments and finite checks; no unavailable earlier kernel or original private source input is reconstructed by this exposition.

**S29.** Orthemology public research. Derivational unification and carrier packaging. Exact Git object: `5b8730ceb698c2a15d8165f23aba851e47f7516f`. The profile-isomorphism argument, product consequence, substantive-unification conditions and bounded application are used. Original executable witnesses and the inherited comparison dossier retain their separate evidence roles. References P23–P26 support the limited literature attributions made here; no original execution or dossier reconstruction is claimed.

**S30.** Orthemology public research. *Isomorphism-natural semantics and interpretation rigidity*, revised public proposal. [Public source edition](https://github.com/theislampill/orthemology/commit/a7ff0e33d1a299db640041c46b1af81ca70851f6). Exact Git object: `757b0914dcbe7ac1319690c639916cc837913ea3`. The selector proof, semantic distinctions and controls are incorporated in §8.10, with the fixed-family direction of signature refinement corrected. Its secondary El-Tobgui attribution remains inherited at that scope, not a newly inspected medieval primary source.

**S31.** Orthemology public research. *Semantic gauge, literal reference and constitutive interpretation*, revised public proposal. [Public source edition](https://github.com/theislampill/orthemology/commit/a7ff0e33d1a299db640041c46b1af81ca70851f6). Exact Git object: `fb0dd5524c9ef2ab6c9c968cf06eedf9cd3e1002`. The content-action obstruction, anchor condition and philosophical and experimental controls are incorporated in §8.10; the distinction between singleton orbits and identifying evidence fibres is explicit. The commuting-action consequence is a composition of these two source treatments, not an assertion of general mathematical novelty or owner adoption.


**S32.** Orthemology public research. *Norm provenance, correction vectors and truth-connected function*. Exact Git object `e07ad6ae52dae67a8b2a341fe5e2c9377a1c9713`, with public intake correction `98781176fe14912cb0ee6d1c541793e4bb906aab` and proposal-status note `c2817d65685bac1984ab631ff0966b406c9afe31`, in the [public source edition](https://github.com/theislampill/orthemology/commit/a7ff0e33d1a299db640041c46b1af81ca70851f6). The vector and its countermodels are source-relative assessment proposals. Sections 7.2–7.3 supply the complete necessary mathematical arguments and retain the source, function, bearer, recipient and execution distinctions. No new execution of the absent historical checker or complete formal certificate is claimed.


**S33.** Orthemology public research. *What the new repair theorems contribute to the source/intentionality frontier*. [Exact public source edition](https://github.com/theislampill/orthemology/blob/b4d58482ae00f138782276ee5c4034c48bb2df19/docs/provenance/v5-research-continuations/notes/21f9e48328bfbf843b5f0371168f84c51768dec6260b3798fca4a461900aa006.md). Exact Git object `b5f6e9140796030880f6dbc723514bfb393d23c2`. Section 7.4 incorporates its compatibility, causal-access, endogenous-function, modal-hosting and bearer-identity arguments, preserving the distinct bridge choices and negative findings. The mathematical antecedents are proved in Common Repair and Information and Inquiry; this synthesis establishes no new kernel result, empirical implementation or source adoption.
